## Summary

Prepared v1.19.0 using the existing bump script after intentional CHANGELOG notes and a read-only remote-tag probe. Updated the full current documentation matrix, provider references/examples/skill update guidance, Azure CLI description, registry-driven model guidance and current counts. Added an executable synthetic route-only example and upstream notices/source-publication exclusions. No runtime admission/collector behavior changed.

This is a worker handoff, not a Done decision or gate PASS. Root still owns the full isolated exact-commit suite, actual test.yml CI/native OS results, new annotated tag and final release record. Live TEST-003/005/009 remain BLOCKED.

Acceptance-first applies (approved risk medium). WFI-001 high-risk committed-Red requirement is not applicable: no new runtime authority/contract behavior is implemented. The actual pre-edit documentation check failed on missing current interfaces and stale claims; previous candidate work is not relabeled unimplemented or retroactive Red.
